````text
{"content": "1|<p align=\"center\">\n2|  <img src=\"hospital_web/public/LOGO.png\" alt=\"LifeLink Logo\" width=\"120\" />\n3|</p>\n4|\n5|<h1 align=\"center\">LifeLink</h1>\n6|\n7|<p align=\"center\">\n8|  <strong>The Ultimate Emergency Blood Donor Ecosystem.</strong><br/>\n9|  Connecting hospitals with donors through military-grade security and instant native notifications.\n10|</p>\n11|\n12|<p align=\"center\">\n13|  <img src=\"https://img.shields.io/badge/Status-Production--Ready-success\" alt=\"Production Ready\" />\n14|  <img src=\"https://img.shields.io/badge/Platform-Web%20%2B%20Mobile-blue\" alt=\"Web + Mobile\" />\n15|  <img src=\"https://img.shields.io/badge/Security-Hardened-red\" alt=\"Security Hardened\" />\n16|</p>\n17|\n18|---\n19|\n20|## 🏗️ Project Architecture\n21|LifeLink is a specialized monorepo designed for scale and security:\n22|\n23|- **[`/hospital_web`](./hospital_web)**: Professional desktop dashboard for medical facility staff.\n24|- **[`/donor_app`](./donor_app)**: Native iOS, Android, and PC application for blood donors.\n25|- **[Shared Backend](./SUPABASE_SETUP_GUIDE.md)**: Hardened Supabase (PostgreSQL) with strict RLS policies.\n26|\n27|---\n28|\n29|## 🚀 Getting Started\n30|\n31|### The Master Controller (One-Command Startup)\n32|LifeLink uses a unified startup script to launch both the web portal and the mobile app simultaneously, while automatically establishing a dynamic IP bridge for your QR codes.\n33|\n34|From the root `LifeLink` directory, simply run:\n35|```bash\n36|npm start\n37|```\n38|*This will automatically launch the Hospital Web Portal (React) and the Donor Mobile App (Expo) in parallel.*\n39|\n40|### Environment Setup\n41|Before starting, ensure you have configured your environment variables using the provided templates:\n42|1. Copy `hospital_web/.env.example` to `hospital_web/.env`\n43|2. Copy `donor_app/.env.example` to `donor_app/.env`\n44|*(The `hospital_web/.env.local` file is generated automatically by the startup script for your IP bridge).*\n45|\n46|---\n47|\n48|## 🔐 Privacy & Security\n49|- **Data Masking**: PII (Personally Identifiable Information) is protected via database-level RLS.\n50|- **Location Jitter**: Donor coordinates are randomized by 200m to protect home addresses.\n51|- **Biometric SecureStore**: Session tokens are hardware-encrypted on mobile devices.\n52|\n53|---\n54|\n55|## 📚 Resource Links\n56|- **[Full Walkthrough](./FEATURES_WALKTHROUGH.md)**: Features & testing guide.\n57|- **[Pentest Report](./SECURITY_PENTEST_REPORT.md)**: Security audit results.\n58|- **[Mobile Plan](./MOBILE_STRATEGY_PLAN.md)**: Roadmap history.\n59|- **[Troubleshooting](./TROUBLESHOOTING.md)**: Help for setup issues.\n60|\n61|---\n62|<p align=\"center\">Saving lives through high-performance engineering. 🩸</p>\n63|", "total_lines": 62, "file_size": 2546, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}
````

````text
{"content": "1|# ⚡ Supabase Setup & Configuration\n2|\n3|This guide ensures your Supabase backend is perfectly configured for the LifeLink monorepo.\n4|\n5|---\n6|\n7|## 1. Database Schema\n8|You must run the SQL in **[`supabase_schema.sql`](file:///hospital_web/supabase_schema.sql)** to set up the following:\n9|\n10|| Table | Purpose | Security |\n11|| :--- | :--- | :--- |\n12|| `profiles` | User roles & metadata | RLS: Owner Only |\n13|| `donors` | Donor records & locations | RLS: Role-Based |\n14|| `hospitals` | Facility details | RLS: Verified Only |\n15|| `blood_requests` | Active emergencies | RLS: Public/Targeted |\n16|| `push_subscriptions` | Web Push tokens | RLS: Owner Only |\n17|\n18|---\n19|\n20|## 2. Authentication Settings\n21|> [!IMPORTANT]\n22|> Enable the following in your Supabase Auth Settings:\n23|1. **Email/Password**: For Hospital staff registration.\n24|2. **Anonymous Sign-ins**: For frictionless Donor onboarding.\n25|3. **Redirect URL**: Set to `http://localhost:3000` (or your production URL).\n26|\n27|---\n28|\n29|## 3. Realtime Enablement\n30|Ensure **Replication** is turned on for the following tables to enable the \"Instant Dashboards\":\n31|- `blood_requests`\n32|- `donor_responses`\n33|- `donors`\n34|\n35|---\n36|\n37|## 4. Edge Functions (Advanced)\n38|To enable real SMS and Push notifications, deploy the functions in the `/supabase/functions` directory:\n39|```bash\n40|supabase functions deploy send-blood-alert\n41|```\n42|\n43|### Required Secrets\n44|Set these in your Supabase dashboard:\n45|- `TWILIO_ACCOUNT_SID`\n46|- `TWILIO_AUTH_TOKEN`\n47|- `TWILIO_FROM_NUMBER`\n48|- `VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY`\n49|\n50|---\n51|\n52|## 5. Security Hardening\n53|> [!CAUTION]\n54|> Always verify that **RLS is ENABLED** for every table. You can check this in the \"Database\" -> \"Tables\" section of your Supabase dashboard.\n55|", "total_lines": 54, "file_size": 1644, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}
````

```text
{"content": "1|-- Life Link Database Schema (Security Hardened)\n2|-- language: pgsql\n3|\n4|-- Donors Table\n5|create table if not exists donors (\n6|  id uuid default gen_random_uuid() primary key,\n7|  user_id uuid references auth.users,\n8|  name text not null,\n9|  blood_group text not null check (blood_group in ('A+','A-','B+','B-','O+','O-','AB+','AB-')),\n10|  city text not null,\n11|  phone text,\n12|  available boolean default true,\n13|  last_donated date,\n14|  total_donations int default 0,\n15|  created_at timestamptz default now()\n16|);\n17|\n18|-- Hospitals Table\n19|create table if not exists hospitals (\n20|  id uuid default gen_random_uuid() primary key,\n21|  user_id uuid references auth.users,\n22|  name text not null,\n23|  city text not null,\n24|  contact_person text,\n25|  phone text,\n26|  is_verified boolean default false, -- Security: prevents fake hospitals from spamming\n27|  created_at timestamptz default now()\n28|);\n29|\n30|-- Profiles Table\n31|create table if not exists profiles (\n32|  user_id uuid primary key references auth.users on delete cascade,\n33|  role text not null check (role in ('hospital','donor')),\n34|  display_name text,\n35|  phone text,\n36|  created_at timestamptz default now()\n37|);\n38|\n39|-- Blood Requests Table\n40|create table if not exists blood_requests (\n41|  id uuid default gen_random_uuid() primary key,\n42|  hospital_id uuid references hospitals(id) on delete cascade,\n43|  blood_group text not null check (blood_group in ('A+','A-','B+','B-','O+','O-','AB+','AB-')),\n44|  component text not null default 'Whole blood'\n45|    check (component in ('Whole blood','Platelets','Plasma','RBC')),\n46|  units_needed int not null default 1,\n47|  urgency text not null default 'Routine'\n48|    check (urgency in ('Routine','Urgent','Critical')),\n49|  status text not null default 'open'\n50|    check (status in ('open','in_progress','filled','cancelled')),\n51|  notes text,\n52|  created_at timestamptz default now(),\n53|  updated_at timestamptz default now()\n54|);\n55|\n56|-- Donor Responses Table\n57|create table if not exists donor_responses (\n58|  id uuid default gen_random_uuid() primary key,\n59|  request_id uuid references blood_requests(id) on delete cascade,\n60|  donor_id uuid references donors(id) on delete cascade,\n61|  status text not null default 'alerted'\n62|    check (status in ('alerted','confirmed','declined','donated')),\n63|  eta_minutes int,\n64|  responded_at timestamptz,\n65|  created_at timestamptz default now(),\n66|  unique(request_id, donor_id)\n67|);\n68|\n69|-- Push Subscriptions Table (for Web Push API)\n70|create table if not exists push_subscriptions (\n71|  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),\n72|  user_id uuid references auth.users on delete cascade,\n73|  subscription jsonb not null,\n74|  created_at timestamptz default now()\n75|);\n76|\n77|-- Realtime Configuration (safe to re-run)\n78|-- Tables are automatically available for realtime if they are in public schema\n79|-- The following ensures they are in the publication (idempotent)\n80|-- NOTE: If you get \"already member\" error, these tables are already configured\n81|\n82|-- Row Level Security (RLS)\n83|alter table profiles        enable row level security;\n84|alter table donors          enable row level security;\n85|alter table hospitals       enable row level security;\n86|alter table blood_requests  enable row level security;\n87|alter table push_subscriptions enable row level security;\n88|alter table donor_responses enable row level security;\n89|\n90|do $$ begin\n91|  create policy \"profiles_owner\" on profiles for all using (\n92|    auth.uid() = user_id\n93|  ) with check (\n94|    auth.uid() = user_id\n95|  );\n96|exception when duplicate_object then null;\n97|end $$;\n98|\n99|-- Donors: users can manage their own record. \n100|-- Hospitals can read basic donor info (name, blood group) for the map, but NOT phone numbers.\n101|-- Phone numbers are ONLY visible to the hospital if the donor has RESPONDED to their request.\n102|drop policy if exists \"donors_owner_manage\" on donors;\n103|drop policy if exists \"hospitals_read_donors\" on donors;\n104|create policy \"donors_owner_manage\" on donors for all using (\n105|  auth.uid() = user_id\n106|);\n107|\n108|create policy \"hospitals_read_basic_donors\" on donors for select using (\n109|  exists (\n110|    select 1 from profiles where user_id = auth.uid() and role = 'hospital'\n111|  )\n112|);\n113|\n114|-- Hospitals: users manage their own.\n115|-- Donors can see hospital list for the directory.\n116|drop policy if exists \"hospitals_owner_manage\" on hospitals;\n117|drop policy if exists \"authenticated_hospitals_view\" on hospitals;\n118|create policy \"hospitals_owner_manage\" on hospitals for all using (\n119|  auth.uid() = user_id\n120|);\n121|\n122|create policy \"donors_read_hospitals\" on hospitals for select using (\n123|  exists (\n124|    select 1 from profiles where user_id = auth.uid() and role = 'donor'\n125|  )\n126|);\n127|\n128|-- Requests: hospitals can manage their own requests; donors can view requests assigned to them.\n129|do $$ begin\n130|  create policy \"hospitals_manage_requests\" on blood_requests for all using (\n131|    exists (\n132|      select 1 from hospitals h where h.id = blood_requests.hospital_id and h.user_id = auth.uid()\n133|    )\n134|  ) with check (\n135|    exists (\n136|      select 1 from hospitals h where h.id = blood_requests.hospital_id and h.user_id = auth.uid()\n137|    )\n138|  );\n139|exception when duplicate_object then null;\n140|end $$;\n141|\n142|do $$ begin\n143|  create policy \"donors_view_assigned_requests\" on blood_requests for select using (\n144|    exists (\n145|      select 1\n146|      from donor_responses dr\n147|      join donors d on d.id = dr.donor_id\n148|      where dr.request_id = blood_requests.id\n149|        and d.user_id = auth.uid()\n150|    )\n151|  );\n152|exception when duplicate_object then This guide explains how to test every major feature in the current monorepo.\n4|\n5|---\n6|\n7|## 🏥 Hospital Web Portal\n8|**Location**: `/hospital_web`\n9|\n10|### 1. Registration & Security\n11|- [ ] **Sign Up**: Create a hospital account with an email and password.\n12|- [ ] **Security**: Your facility must be marked `is_verified: true` in the Supabase database to send real alerts.\n13|\n14|### 2. Posting Requests\n15|- [ ] **Emergency Feed**: Create a \"Critical\" request for B+ blood.\n16|- [ ] **Real-time Patching**: Notice the dashboard updates instantly without a page refresh.\n17|\n18|### 3. Response Management\n19|- [ ] **Donor Tracking**: Watch the \"Active Responses\" tab update as donors respond via the app.\n20|\n21|---\n22|\n23|## 📱 Donor Native App (Phase 4)\n24|**Location**: `/donor_app`\n25|\n26|### 1. Native Identity & Security\n27|- ✅ **SecureStore**: Session tokens are hardware-encrypted.\n28|- ✅ **Biometrics**: Ready for FaceID/Fingerprint integration.\n29|\n30|### 2. Ergonomic Dashboard\n31|- ✅ **Thumb-Zone UI**: High-accessibility navigation for all ages.\n32|- ✅ **Emergency Feed**: Real-time hospital requests delivered instantly.\n33|\n34|### 3. Native Mapping\n35|- ✅ **GPU Accelerated**: Liquid-smooth interactive map.\n36|- ✅ **Privacy Jitter**: Hardware-enforced location anonymization.\n37|\n38|### 4. Native Alerts\n39|- ✅ **Push Notifications**: System-level alerts that arrive even when app is closed.\n40|- ✅ **Test Mode**: One-tap native verification button.\n41|\n42|---\n43|\n44|## 🧪 Testing the \"WOW\" Factors\n45|\n46|### The Simulation Mode\n47|> [!TIP]\n48|> Use these steps to demo the platform without setting up real SMS or Mapbox accounts.\n49|\n50|| Feature | How to Test |\n51|| :--- | :--- |\n52|| **Native Push** | Click **\"Send Test Push\"** in the Donor Hub. |\n53|| **SMS Logic** | Create a request and check the **Browser Console (F12)** for mock alert logs. |\n54|| **Privacy Map** | Zoom into any donor marker on the map to see the 200m randomized offset. |\n55|\n56|---\n57|\n58|## 🛡️ Security Audit Note\n59|All features listed above are protected by **Row-Level Security (RLS)**. }} \n41|            />\n42|          </div>\n43|          <h1 style={{ fontSize: '20px', letterSpacing: '-0.5px', fontWeight: 800 }}>LifeLink</h1>\n44|        </Link>\n45|        \n46|        <div className=\"nav-actions\">\n47|          <div className=\"theme-dock\">\n48|            <button onClick={() => setTheme('light')} className={`theme-btn ${theme === 'light' ? 'active' : ''}`} title=\"Light Mode\"><Sun size={14} /></button>\n49|            <button onClick={() => setTheme('dark')} className={`theme-btn ${theme === 'dark' ? 'active' : ''}`} title=\"Dark Mode\"><Moon size={14} /></button>\n50|            <button onClick={() => setTheme('system')} className={`theme-btn ${theme === 'system' ? 'active' : ''}`} title=\"System Mode\"><Monitor size={14} /></button>\n51|          </div>\n52|          {session && (\n53|            <>\n54|              {isAdmin && (\n55|                <a href=\"/admin\" className=\"nav-item text-accent\" style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '6px', textDecoration: 'none' }}>\n56|                  <ShieldCheck size={16} /> Control Panel\n57|                </a>\n58|              )}\n59|              <button onClick={handleLogout} className=\"nav-item\" title=\"Sign Out\"><LogOut size={16} /> Sign Out</button>\n60|            </>\n61|          )}\n62|        </div>\n63|      </header>\n64|\n65|      {children}\n66|    </div>\n67|  )\n68|}\n69|\n70|export default function App() {\n71|  const [session, setSession] = useState(null)\n72|  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)\n73|  const { isAdmin } = useAdmin(session?.user)\n74|\n75|  useEffect(() => {\n76|    supabase.auth.getSession().then(({ data: { session } }) => {\n77|      setSession(session)\n78|      setLoading(false)\n79|    })\n80|    const { data: { subscription } } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange((_event, session) => {\n81|      setSession(session)\n82|    })\n83|    return () => subscription.unsubscribe()\n84|  }, [])\n85|\n86|  // Explicitly handle anonymous sign-in for donors if no session exists\n87|  const handleDonorAccess = async () => {\n88|    if (!session) {\n89|      const { data, error } = await supabase.auth.signInAnonymously()\n90|      if (error) console.error(\"Anonymous auth failed:\", error)\n91|      return data.session\n92|    }\n93|    return session\n94|  }\n95|\n96|  if (loading) return null\n97|\n98|  return (\n99|    <BrowserRouter>\n100|      <AppLayout session={session} isAdmin={isAdmin}>\n101|        <Routes>\n102|          <Route path=\"/\" element={\n103|            session ? <Auth /> : (isAdmin ? <AdminDashboard user={session.user} /> : <Navigate to=\"/hospital\" replace />)\n117|          } />\n118|          \n119|          <Route path=\"*\" element={<Navigate to=\"/\" replace />} />\n120|        </Routes>\n121|      </AppLayout>\n122|    </BrowserRouter>\n123|  )\n124|}", "total_lines": 123, "file_size": 5002, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}
```

## Assistant - 2026-08-23T14:33:35Z

Based on my exploration, **LifeLink** is a production-ready emergency blood donor ecosystem with a monorepo architecture:

## Project Structure
```
LifeLink/
├── hospital_web/          # React 18 + Supabase dashboard for hospitals
├── donor_app/             # Expo 55 (React Native) mobile app for donors
├── scripts/               # Startup scripts (IP detection, concurrent launch)
└── supabase_schema.sql    # Hardened PostgreSQL schema with full RLS
```

## Key Architecture Points

**Hospital Web (`hospital_web/`)**  
- React 18 + React Router + Supabase JS client  
- Real-time subscriptions via Supabase Realtime  
- Maps via MapLibre GL / Mapbox GL  
- Components: `HospitalDashboard`, `DonorDashboard`, `AdminDashboard`, `Auth`, `LandingPage`

**Donor Mobile App (`donor_app/`)**  
- Expo Router (file-based routing) with tabs: **Alerts / Map / Admin**  
- Native features: SecureStore (hardware-encrypted tokens), Biometrics, Push Notifications, Location (with 200m privacy jitter), Haptics  
- Custom hooks: `useDonorAuth`, `useRequests`, `usePushNotifications`

**Database (PostgreSQL + RLS)**  
Tables: `profiles`, `donors`, `hospitals`, `blood_requests`, `donor_responses`, `push_subscriptions`  
- All tables have RLS enabled with role-based policies  
- Hospitals see donor basic info (no phone) unless donor responds to their request  
- Anonymous auth for donors, email/password for hospitals

## Quick Start
```bash
cd C:/LifeLink
# 1. Configure env files
cp hospital_web/.env.example hospital_web/.env
cp donor_app/.env.example donor_app/.env

# 2. Run supabase_schema.sql in your Supabase project

# 3. Start both apps
npm start
```

## What would you like to do next?
- **Run/test** the applications
- **Add features** (new dashboard components, notification logic, etc.)
- **Debug** specific issues
- **Deploy** configuration
- **Review/optimize** code quality or security

```
1import { createServerClient } from "@supabase/ssr";
2import { cookies } from "next/headers";
3
4const supabaseUrl = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL;
5const supabaseKey = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY;
6
7export const createClient = (cookieStore: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof cookies>>) => {
8  return createServerClient(
9    supabaseUrl!,
10    supabaseKey!,
11    {
12      cookies: {
13        getAll() {
14          return cookieStore.getAll()
15        },
16        setAll(cookiesToSet) {
17          try {
18            cookiesToSet.forEach(({ name, value, options }) => cookieStore.set(name, value, options))
19          } catch {
20            // The `setAll` method was called from a Server Component.
21            // This can be ignored if you have middleware refreshing
22            // user sessions.
23          }
24        },
25      },
26    },
27  );
28};
```

File: utils/supabase/client.ts

File: utils/supabase/middleware.ts
```
1import { createServerClient } from "@supabase/ssr";
2import { type NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
3
4const supabaseUrl = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL;
5const supabaseKey = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY;
6
7export const createClient = (request: NextRequest) => {
8  // Create an unmodified response
9  let supabaseResponse = NextResponse.next({
10    request: {
11      headers: request.headers,
12    },
13  });
14
15  const supabase = createServerClient(
16    supabaseUrl!,
17    supabaseKey!,
18    {
19      cookies: {
20        getAll() {
21          return request.cookies.getAll()
22        },
23        setAll(cookiesToSet) {
24          cookiesToSet.forEach(({ name, value, options }) => request.cookies.set(name, value))
25          supabaseResponse = NextResponse.next({
26            request,
27          })
28          cookiesToSet.forEach(({ name, value, options }) =>
29            supabaseResponse.cookies.set(name, value, options)
30          )
31        },
32      },
33    },
34  );
35
36  return supabaseResponse
37};
```

## Assistant - 2026-08-23T14:36:30Z

The instructions you've shared are for a **Next.js** project (using `@supabase/ssr`, middleware, `next/headers`, Server Components), but **LifeLink is not a Next.js project** — it's a monorepo with:

- **`hospital_web/`** → React 18 (Create React App / `react-scripts`)
- **`donor_app/`** → Expo 55 (React Native)

Neither uses Next.js, so `@supabase/ssr`, middleware, and the `utils/supabase/` pattern don't apply here.

## Current Supabase Setup in LifeLink

**Hospital Web** (`hospital_web/src/lib/supabase.js` — already exists):
```js
import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'

const supabaseUrl = process.env.REACT_APP_SUPABASE_URL
const supabaseKey = process.env.REACT_APP_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY

export const supabase = createClient(supabaseUrl, supabaseKey)
```

**Donor App** (`donor_app/lib/supabase.ts` — already exists):
```ts
import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'

const supabaseUrl = process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL
const supabaseKey = process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY

export const supabase = createClient(supabaseUrl, supabaseKey)
```

## What You Actually Need

1. **Env files** (already have `.env.example` templates):
   ```bash
   cp hospital_web/.env.example hospital_web/.env
   cp donor_app/.env.example donor_app/.env
   ```
   Then fill in your Supabase URL and anon/publishable key.

2. **Run the schema** — execute `hospital_web/supabase_schema.sql` in your Supabase SQL editor.

3. **Enable Auth providers** in Supabase dashboard:
   - Email/Password (for hospitals)
   - Anonymous Sign-ins (for donors)

4. **Start the apps**:
   ```bash
   cd C:/LifeLink
   npm run install-all   # first time only
   npm start             # launches both apps
   ```

## Assistant - 2026-08-23T14:37:11Z

These instructions are for **traditional backend servers** (Node.js, Express, Fastify, etc.) or **Edge Functions** that need to verify JWTs and act as a resource server. 

**LifeLink doesn't need this** — here's why:

## LifeLink's Architecture = Client-Side Supabase Only

| Layer | Technology | Supabase Usage |
|-------|------------|----------------|
| **Hospital Web** | React 18 (CRA) | `@supabase/supabase-js` directly in browser |
| **Donor App** | Expo 55 (React Native) | `@supabase/supabase-js` directly on device |
| **Backend Logic** | **Supabase itself** | Postgres + RLS + Realtime + Auth + Edge Functions |

## When `@supabase/server` Would Be Used

Only if you build a **custom API server** that:
- Sits between your clients and Supabase
- Verifies JWTs from clients before proxying requests
- Needs to call Supabase Admin APIs (service role)

## What LifeLink Actually Uses for "Server" Logic

**Supabase Edge Functions** (in `hospital_web/supabase/functions/`):
```bash
# Deploy with (no npm install needed - @supabase/server is built-in)
supabase functions deploy send-blood-alert
```

These have `@supabase/server` available automatically and receive the user's JWT via `Authorization` header.

---

## What You Should Do Instead

1. **Configure your existing `.env` files** (already have templates):
   ```bash
   # hospital_web/.env
   REACT_APP_SUPABASE_URL=https://hqdiqjswknukqyghtgsa.supabase.co
   REACT_APP_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY=sb_pub...H3CT
   
   # donor_app/.env
   EXPO_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL=https://hqdiqjswknukqyghtgsa.supabase.co
   EXPO_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY=sb_pub...H3CT
   ```

2. **Run the schema** in Supabase SQL editor:
   ```sql
   -- Copy/paste from hospital_web/supabase_schema.sql
   ```

3. **Enable Auth providers** in Supabase Dashboard → Authentication → Providers:
   - ✅ Email/Password
   - ✅ Anonymous Sign-ins

4. **Deploy Edge Functions** (if you need SMS/push):
   ```bash
   cd hospital_web
   supabase functions deploy send-blood-alert
   # Set secrets: TWILIO_*, VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY
   ```

5. **Start the apps**:
   ```bash
   cd C:/LifeLink
   npm run install-all
   npm start
   ```

'../lib/bloodLogic'\n9|import {\n10|  AlertCircle, Clock, CheckCircle, Plus, X,\n11|  Activity, ChevronDown, ChevronUp, MapPin, \n12|  Droplets, Users, HeartPulse, Phone, Heart\n13|} from 'lucide-react'\n14|import { QRCodeCanvas } from 'qrcode.react'\n15|import { CardSkeleton } from './Skeleton'\n16|import toast from 'react-hot-toast'\n17|\n18|const BLOOD_GROUPS  = ['A+', 'A-', 'B+', 'B-', 'O+', 'O-', 'AB+', 'AB-']\n19|const COMPONENTS    = ['Whole blood', 'Platelets', 'Plasma', 'RBC']\n20|const URGENCY_OPTS  = ['Routine', 'Urgent', 'Critical']\n21|\n22|const URGENCY_BADGES = {\n23|  Routine:  'badge-low',\n24|  Urgent:   'badge-mid',\n25|  Critical: 'badge-high',\n26|}\n27|\n28|const STATUS_LABELS = {\n29|  open:        'Open',\n30|  in_progress: 'In progress',\n31|  filled:      'Filled',\n32|  cancelled:   'Cancelled',\n33|}\n34|\n35|function timeAgo(ts) {\n36|  const diff = Math.floor((Date.now() - new Date(ts)) / 1000)\n37|  if (diff < 60)   return `${diff}s ago`\n38|  if (diff < 3600) return `${Math.floor(diff / 60)}m ago`\n39|  return `${Math.floor(diff / 3600)}h ago`\n40|}\n41|\n42|function NewRequestModal({ myHospital, onClose, refetch, mutateRequests }) {\n43|  const { donors } = useDonors()\n44|  const [form, setForm] = useState({\n45|    hospital_id:  myHospital.id,\n46|    blood_group:  'O+',\n47|    component:    'Whole blood',\n48|    units_needed: 1,\n49|    urgency:      'Critical',\n50|    delivery_point: '',\n51|    notes:        '',\n52|  })\n53|  const [loading, setLoading]     = useState(false)\n54|\n55|  const handleSubmit = async () => {\n56|    if (!form.hospital_id) return\n57|    setLoading(true)\n58|    const { data, error } = await supabase\n59|      .from('blood_requests')\n60|      .insert([{ \n61|        ...form, \n62|        units_needed: Number(form.units_needed),\n63|        delivery_point: form.delivery_point || 'General Admissions',\n64|        status: 'open'\n65|      }])\n66|      .select().single()\n67|\n68|    if (!error && data) {\n69|      const compatibleGroups = getCompatibleDonorGroups(form.blood_group)\n70|      const matched = donors.filter(d => compatibleGroups.includes(d.blood_group) && d.available)\n71|      if (matched.length > 0) {\n72|        await supabase.from('donor_responses').insert(\n73|          matched.map(d => ({ request_id: data.id, donor_id: d.id, status: 'alerted' }))\n74|        )\n75|\n76|        // Trigger SMS Alerts for Urgent/Critical requests\n77|        if (['Urgent', 'Critical'].includes(form.urgency)) {\n78|          console.log('📡 Trigger the Edge Function (SMS/Push logic)')\n79|          const { data: funcData, error: funcError } = await supabase.functions.invoke('send-blood-alert', {\n80|            body: { \n81|              requestId: data.id,\n82|              hospitalName: myHospital.name,\n83|              bloodGroup: form.blood_group,\n84|              urgency: form.urgency\n85|            }\n86|          })\n87|\n88|          if (funcError) {\n89|            console.warn('📡 Simulation Mode: Edge function trigger failed (likely due to missing Twilio keys).')\n90|            console.log(`💡 [MOCK ALERT] An emergency SMS would have been sent to matching ${form.blood_group} donors in ${myHospital.city}.`)\n91|          } else {\n92|            console.log('Emergency alerts sent successfully to matching donors!')\n93|          }\n94|        }\n95|      }\n96|      \n97|      // Optimistic UI Update: Instantly inject the new request\n98|      if (mutateRequests) {\n99|        mutateRequests(prev => [{ ...data, hospitals: myHospital, donor_responses: [] }, ...prev])\n100|      }\n101|      if (refetch) refetch(true)\n102|      onClose()\n103|    }\n104|    setLoading(false)\n105|  }\n106|\n107|  const compatibleGroups = getCompatibleDonorGroups(form.blood_group)\n108|  const matchedDonors = donors.filter(d => compatibleGroups.includes(d.blood_group) && d.available).length\n109|\n110|  return (\n111|    <div className=\"modal-overlay\">\n112|      <div className=\"modal-content ui-panel fade-in\">\n113|        <div className=\"flex-between\" style={{ marginBottom: '40px' }}>\n114|          <h2 style={{ fontSize: '28px' }}>Emergency Request</h2>\n115|          <button onClick={onClose} className=\"btn-ghost\" style={{ padding: '12px', borderRadius: '50%' }}><X size={20} /></button>\n116|        </div>\n117|\n118|        <div className=\"form-field\">\n119|          <label className=\"field-label\">Target Blood Group</label>\n120|          <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(4, 1fr)', gap: '10px' }}>\n121|            {BLOOD_GROUPS.map(bg => (\n122|              <button key={bg} onClick={() => setForm(f => ({ ...f, blood_group: bg }))}\n123|                className={`btn-ghost ${form.blood_group === bg ? 'btn-solid' : ''}`}\n124|                style={{ padding: '12px 0', fontSize: '13px' }}>\n125|                {bg}\n126|              </button>\n127|            ))}\n128|          </div>\n129|          {matchedDonors > 0 && (\n130|            <div style={{ marginTop: '16px', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px', color: 'var(--accent-green)', fontSize: '14px', fontWeight: 600 }}>\n131|              <Users size={16} /> {matchedDonors} compatible donors ready\n132|            </div>\n133|          )}\n134|        </div>\n135|\n136|        <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: '20px' }}>\n137|          <div className=\"form-field\">\n138|            <label className=\"field-label\">Component</label>\n139|            <select className=\"text-input\" style={{ height: '52px' }} value={form.component} onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, component: e.target.value }))}>\n140|              {COMPONENTS.map(c => <option key={c}>{c}</option>)}\n141|            </select>\n142|          </div>\n143|          <div className=\"form-field\">\n144|            <label className=\"field-label\">Units (Gloved-Friendly)</label>\n145|            <div className=\"flex-center\" style={{ gap: '0', background: 'var(--bg-app)', padding: '2px', borderRadius: '12px', border: '1px solid var(--border-soft)', height: '52px' }}>\n146|              <button onClick={() => setForm(f => ({ ...f, units_needed: Math.max(1, f.units_needed - 1) }))} className=\"btn-ghost\" style={{ flex: 1, height: '100%', fontSize: '20px' }}>-</button>\n147|              <span style={{ fontSize: '20px', fontWeight: 800, minWidth: '50px', textAlign: 'center' }}>{form.units_needed}</span>\n148|              <button onClick={() => setForm(f => ({ ...f, units_needed: f.units_needed + 1 }))} className=\"btn-ghost\" style={{ flex: 1, height: '100%', fontSize: '20px' }}>+</button>\n149|            </div>\n150|          </div>\n151|        </div>\n152|\n153|        <div className=\"form-field\">\n154|          <label className=\"field-label\">Precise Delivery Point (Ward / Room / ER Entrance)</label>\n155|          <input className=\"text-input\" placeholder=\"e.g. ICU 2nd Floor, Room 204\" value={form.delivery_point} onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, delivery_point: e.target.value }))} />\n156|        </div>\n157|\n158|        <div className=\"form-field\">\n159|          <label className=\"field-label\">Urgency</label>\n160|          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '10px' }}>\n161|            {URGENCY_OPTS.map(u => (\n162|              <button key={u} onClick={() => setForm(f => ({ ...f, urgency: u }))}\n163|                className={`btn-ghost ${form.urgency === u ? 'btn-solid' : ''}`} style={{ flex: 1 }}>{u}</button>\n164|            ))}\n165|          </div>\n166|        </div>\n167|\n168|        <div className=\"form-field\" style={{ marginBottom: '40px' }}>\n169|          <label className=\"field-label\">Clinical Notes</label>\n170|          <input className=\"text-input\" placeholder=\"e.g. Operation Room 4, Attn: Dr. Smith\" value={form.notes} onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, notes: e.target.value }))} />\n171|        </div>\n172|\n173|        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '16px' }}>\n174|          <button onClick={onClose} className=\"btn-ghost\" style={{ flex: 1 }}>Cancel</button>\n175|          <button onClick={handleSubmit} disabled={loading} className=\"btn-solid\" style={{ flex: 2 }}>{loading ? 'Transmitting...' : 'Post Emergency Alert'}</button>\n176|        </div>\n177|      </div>\n178|    </div>\n179|  )\n180|}\n181|\n182|export default function HospitalDashboard({ user }) {\n183|  const [myHospital, setMyHospital] = useState(null)\n184|  const [profileLoading, setProfileLoading] = useState(true)\n185|  const { requests, loading, refetch, mutateRequests } = useRequests()\n186|  const { donors } = useDonors()\n187|  const [showModal, setShowModal]   = useState(false)\n188|  const [expandedId, setExpandedId] = useState(null)\n189|  const [activeTab, setActiveTab]   = useState('emergencies')\n190|  const [selectedDonor, setSelectedDonor] = useState(null)\n191|  const { hospitals } = useHospitals()\n192|  const historyRequests = useMemo(() => requests.filter(r => r.hospital_id === myHospital?.id && r.status === 'closed'), [requests, myHospital])\n193|  const activeRequests = useMemo(() => requests.filter(r => r.hospital_id === myHospital?.id && r.status !== 'closed'), [requests, myHospital])\n194|\n195|  useEffect(() => {\n196|    async function loadProfile() {\n197|      if (!user) return\n198|      const { data } = await supabase.from('hospitals').select('*').eq('user_id', user.id).single()\n199|      if (data) setMyHospital(data)\n200|      setProfileLoading(false)\n201|    }\n202|    loadProfile()\n203|  }, [user])\n204|\n205|  const updateStatus = async (id, newStatus) => {\n206|    let receivedBy = null\n207|    if (newStatus === 'closed') {\n208|      receivedBy = window.prompt('Enter staff name who received the blood:')\n209|      if (!receivedBy) return // Cancel if no name\n210|    }\n211|    \n212|    const { error } = await supabase\n213|      .from('blood_requests')\n214|      .update({ status: newStatus, notes: receivedBy ? `Received by: ${receivedBy}` : undefined })\n215|      .eq('id', id)\n216|      \n217|    if (error) toast.error('Update failed')\n218|    else {\n219|      toast.success('Request updated')\n220|      // Optimistic Update\n221|      mutateRequests(prev => prev.map(r => r.id === id ? { ...r, status: newStatus, notes: receivedBy ? `Received by: ${receivedBy}` : r.notes } : r))\n222|      refetch(true)\n223|    }\n224|  }\n225|\n226|  const myRequests = useMemo(() => requests.filter(r => r.hospital_id === myHospital?.id), [requests, myHospital])\n227|  \n228|  const stats = useMemo(() => ({\n229|    open:       activeRequests.filter(r => r.status === 'open').length,\n230|    inProgress: activeRequests.filter(r => r.status === 'in_progress').length,\n231|    filled:     historyRequests.length,\n232|    alerted:    activeRequests.reduce((acc, r) => acc + (r.donor_responses?.length || 0), 0)\n233|  }), [activeRequests, historyRequests])\n234|\n235|  if (profileLoading) return <div className=\"flex-center\" style={{ height: '80vh', justifyContent: 'center', fontSize: '18px', fontWeight: 600 }}>Initialising Secure Medical Node...</div>\n236|\n237|  if (!myHospital) {\n238|    return (\n239|      <div className=\"flex-center\" style={{ minHeight: '90vh', justifyContent: 'center' }}>\n240|        <form className=\"ui-panel fade-in\" style={{ padding: '60px', maxWidth: '500px', width: '90%' }} \n241|          onSubmit={async (e) => {\n242|            e.preventDefault()\n243|            const fd = new FormData(e.target)\n244|            \n245|            // Capture coordinates if available\n246|            let lat = null, lng = null;\n247|            try {\n248|              const pos = await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\n249|                navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(resolve, reject, { timeout: 5000 });\n250|              });\n251|              lat = pos.coords.latitude;\n252|              lng = pos.coords.longitude;\n253|            } catch (err) {\n254|              console.warn(\"📡 Geolocation failed, proceeding with city only:\", err);\n255|            }\n256|\n257|            const { data } = await supabase.from('hospitals').insert([{ \n258|              user_id: user.id, \n259|              name: fd.get('name'), \n260|              city: fd.get('city'), \n261|              contact_person: fd.get('contact'), \n262|              phone: fd.get('phone'),\n263|              latitude: lat,\n264|              longitude: lng\n265|            }]).select().single()\n266|            if (data) setMyHospital(data)\n267|          }}>\n268|          <h1 style={{ marginBottom: '12px', fontSize: '32px' }}>Hospital Setup</h1>\n269|          <p style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)', marginBottom: '40px' }}>Register your medical facility to the LifeLink network.</p>\n270|          <div className=\"form-field\"><label className=\"field-label\">Facility Name</label><input name=\"name\" required className=\"text-input\" /></div>\n271|          <div className=\"form-field\"><label className=\"field-label\">City</label><input name=\"city\" required className=\"text-input\" /></div>\n272|          <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: '20px', marginBottom: '40px' }}>\n273|            <div className=\"form-field\"><label className=\"field-label\">Contact Person</label><input name=\"contact\" required className=\"text-input\" /></div>\n274|            <div className=\"form-field\"><label className=\"field-label\">Phone</label><input name=\"phone\" required className=\"text-input\" /></div>\n275|          </div>\n276|          \n277|          <button type=\"submit\" className=\"btn-solid\" style={{ width: '100%' }}>\n278|            Register Facility\n279|          </button>\n280|        </form>\n281|      </div>\n282|    )\n283|  }\n284|\n285|  return (\n286|    <div className=\"dashboard-page fade-in\">\n287|      <header className=\"header-section\">\n288|        <div>\n289|          <h1 style={{ fontSize: '42px', marginBottom: '8px' }}>{myHospital.name}</h1>\n290|          <div className=\"flex-center\" style={{ gap: '16px', color: 'var(--text-muted)', fontSize: '15px' }}>\n291|            <span className=\"flex-center\" style={{ gap: '6px' }}><MapPin size={16} /> {myHospital.city}</span>\n292|            <span className=\"flex-center\" style={{ gap: '6px' }}><HeartPulse size={16} /> Network Active</span>\n293|          </div>\n294|        </div>\n295|        <button onClick={() => setShowModal(true)} className=\"btn-solid\">\n296|          <Plus size={20} /> New Request\n297|        </button>\n298|      </header>\n299|\n300|      <div className=\"stats-grid\">\n301|        <div className=\"ui-panel stat-card\">\n302|          <div className=\"stat-value\" style={{ color: 'var(--accent-red)' }}>{stats.open}</div>\n303|          <div className=\"stat-label\">Critical Needs</div>\n304|        </div>\n305|        <div className=\"ui-panel stat-card\">\n306|          <div className=\"stat-value\" style={{ color: 'var(--accent-orange)' }}>{stats.inProgress}</div>\n307|          <div className=\"stat-label\">In Transit</div>\n308|        </div>\n309|        <div className=\"ui-panel stat-card\">\n310|          <div className=\"stat-value\" style={{ color: 'var(--accent-green)' }}>{stats.filled}</div>\n311|          <div className=\"stat-label\">Lives Saved</div>\n312|        </div>\n313|        <div className=\"ui-panel stat-card\">\n314|          <div className=\"stat-value\" style={{ color: 'var(--accent-blue)' }}>{stats.alerted}</div>\n315|          <div className=\"stat-label\">Network Alerts</div>\n316|        </div>\n317|        \n318|        {/* Mobile App Promotion for Staff */}\n319|        <div className=\"ui-panel stat-card flex-center\" style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: '16px', padding: '12px 20px', border: '1px solid var(--accent-blue)' }}>\n320|           <div style={{ background: 'white', padding: '4px', borderRadius: '4px', display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'center' }}>\n321|              <QRCodeCanvas value={process.env.REACT_APP_EXPO_URL || 'exp://localhost:8081'} size={40} level=\"L\" />\n322|           </div>\n323|           <div style={{ textAlign: 'left' }}>\n324|              <div style={{ fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 700 }}>Expo Bridge</div>\n325|              <div style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>Scan for Expo Go</div>\n326|           </div>\n327|        </div>\n328|      </div>\n329|\n330|      <div className=\"view-tabs\">\n331|        <button className={`tab-btn ${activeTab === 'emergencies' ? 'active' : ''}`} onClick={() => setActiveTab('emergencies')}>\n332|          <Activity size={18} /> Active Emergencies\n333|        </button>\n334|        <button className={`tab-btn ${activeTab === 'map' ? 'active' : ''}`} onClick={() => setActiveTab('map')}>\n335|          <MapPin size={18} /> Network Map\n336|        </button>\n337|        <button className={`tab-btn ${activeTab === 'directory' ? 'active' : ''}`} onClick={() => setActiveTab('directory')}>\n338|          <Users size={18} /> Donor Directory\n339|        </button>\n340|        <button className={`tab-btn ${activeTab === 'history' ? 'active' : ''}`} onClick={() => setActiveTab('history')}>\n341|          <Clock size={18} /> Past Deliveries\n342|        </button>\n343|      </div>\n344|\n345|      {activeTab === 'emergencies' && (\n346|        <section className=\"fade-in\">\n347|          <div className=\"flex-between\" style={{ marginBottom: '32px' }}>\n348|            <h2 style={{ fontSize: '24px' }}>Active Emergency Stream</h2>\n349|            {loading && <div style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>Syncing...</div>}\n350|          </div>\n351|          \n352|          <div className=\"stream-container\">\n353|            {loading ? (\n354|              <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fill, minmax(300px, 1fr))', gap: '20px' }}>\n355|                {[1, 2, 3].map(i => <CardSkeleton key={i} />)}\n356|              </div>\n357|            ) : myRequests.length === 0 ? (\n358|              <div className=\"ui-panel flex-center\" style={{ padding: '80px', justifyContent: 'center', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '20px', borderStyle: 'dashed', background: 'transparent' }}>\n359|                <Heart size={48} strokeWidth={1} style={{ color: 'var(--accent-red)' }} />\n360|                <h3 style={{ fontSize: '20px', fontWeight: 600 }}>System Secure & Idle</h3>\n361|                <p style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)', fontWeight: 500, textAlign: 'center', maxWidth: '350px' }}>\n362|                  There are no active blood requests at this time. Use the \"New Request\" button to alert donors.\n363|                </p>\n364|              </div>\n365|            ) : (\n366|              activeRequests.map(req => (\n367|              <div key={req.id} className={`ui-panel data-row ${expandedId === req.id ? 'active' : ''}`} onClick={() => setExpandedId(expandedId === req.id ? null : req.id)} style={{ flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'stretch' }}>\n368|                <div className=\"flex-between\" style={{ width: '100%' }}>\n369|                  <div className=\"flex-center\" style={{ gap: '24px' }}>\n370|                    <div className=\"blood-tag\">{req.blood_group}</div>\n371|                    <div>\n372|                      <div style={{ fontWeight: 700, fontSize: '18px' }}>{req.component}</div>\n373|                      <div style={{ fontSize: '14px', color: 'var(--text-muted)', marginTop: '4px' }}>\n374|                        {req.units_needed} Units Required • {req.delivery_point ? `Deliver to: ${req.delivery_point}` : timeAgo(req.created_at)}\n375|                      </div>\n376|                    </div>\n377|                  </div>\n378|                  <div className=\"flex-center\" style={{ gap: '16px' }}>\n379|                    <span className={`badge ${URGENCY_BADGES[req.urgency]}`}>{req.urgency}</span>\n380|                    <div className=\"badge badge-low\" style={{ background: 'var(--bg-app)' }}>{STATUS_LABELS[req.status]}</div>\n381|                    {expandedId === req.id ? <ChevronUp size={20} /> : <ChevronDown size={20} />}\n382|                  </div>\n383|                </div>\n384|\n385|                {expandedId === req.id && (\n386|                  <div style={{ width: '100%', marginTop: '24px', paddingTop: '24px', borderTop: '1px solid var(--border-soft)' }}>\n387|                     {req.notes && <p style={{ fontSize: '15px', color: 'var(--text-muted)', marginBottom: '32px', fontStyle: 'italic' }}>\"{req.notes}\"</p>}\n388|                     <h4 style={{ marginBottom: '16px', fontSize: '14px', textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.1em', color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>Donor Response Network</h4>\n389|                     {req.donor_responses?.length > 0 ? (\n390|                        <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fill, minmax(200px, 1fr))', gap: '12px' }}>\n391|                          {req.donor_responses.map(dr => (\n392|                            <div key={dr.id} className=\"ui-panel\" style={{ padding: '16px', background: 'var(--bg-app)', borderRadius: '16px' }}>\n393|                              <div style={{ fontWeight: 700, fontSize: '14px' }}>{dr.donors?.name}</div>\n394|                              <div className=\"flex-between\" style={{ marginTop: '8px' }}>\n395|                                <div style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: dr.status === 'accepted' ? 'var(--accent-green)' : 'var(--text-muted)', fontWeight: 600 }}>{dr.status.toUpperCase()}</div>\n396|                                {dr.status === 'accepted' && dr.donors?.phone && (\n397|                                  <a href={`tel:${dr.donors.phone}`} className=\"btn-solid\" style={{ padding: '4px 8px', fontSize: '11px', borderRadius: '8px', textDecoration: 'none' }}>\n398|                                    <Phone size={12} /> CALL\n399|                                  </a>\n400|                                )}\n401|                              </div>\n402|                            </div>\n403|                          ))}\n404|                        </div>\n405|                     ) : <p style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)', fontSize: '14px' }}>Waiting for donor responses...</p>}\n406|                      <div style={{ marginTop: '24px', display: 'flex', gap: '12px' }}>\n407|                        {req.status === 'open' && (\n408|                          <button className=\"btn-solid\" style={{ background: 'var(--accent-orange)' }} onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); updateStatus(req.id, 'in_progress'); }}>\n409|                            Mark In Progress\n410|                          </button>\n411|                        )}\n412|                        {req.status !== 'closed' && (\n413|                          <button className=\"btn-solid\" style={{ background: 'var(--accent-green)' }} onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); updateStatus(req.id, 'closed'); }}>\n414|                            Complete Request\n415|                          </button>\n416|                        )}\n417|                        <button className=\"btn-ghost\" onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); updateStatus(req.id, 'cancelled'); }}>Cancel Request</button>\n418|                      </div>\n419|                  </div>\n420|                )}\n421|              </div>\n422|            ))\n423|          )}\n424|        </div>\n425|      </section>\n426|    )}\n427|\n428|      {activeTab === 'map' && (\n429|        <section className=\"fade-in\">\n430|          <div style={{ marginBottom: '32px' }}>\n431|            <h2 style={{ fontSize: '24px' }}>Real-time Emergency Intel</h2>\n432|            <p style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)', marginTop: '4px' }}>Visualising hospital needs and donor distribution across the network.</p>\n433|          </div>\n434|          <EmergencyMap donors={donors} hospitals={hospitals} requests={requests} />\n435|        </section>\n436|      )}\n437|\n438|      {activeTab === 'directory' && (\n439|        <section>\n440|          <div className=\"flex-between\" style={{ marginBottom: '32px' }}>\n441|            <div>\n442|              <h2 style={{ fontSize: '24px' }}>Donor Command Center</h2>\n443|              <p style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)', marginTop: '4px' }}>Manage and contact the volunteer donor network.</p>\n444|            </div>\n445|            <div className=\"flex-center\" style={{ gap: '12px' }}>\n446|               <select \n447|                 className=\"text-input\" \n448|                 style={{ width: '150px', padding: } catch (error) {\n70|      console.error('❌ Failed to subscribe:', error)\n71|    }\n72|  }, [user])\n73|\n74|  useEffect(() => {\n75|    if (user && permission === 'default') {\n76|      // We don't 'react-native';\n5|import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';\n6|import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';\n7|\n8|const isExpoGo = Constants.appOwnership === 'expo' || Constants.executionEnvironment === 'storeClient';\n9|\n10|// Configure how notifications behave when the app is OPEN\n11|// Gate by isExpoGo since Expo SDK 53 removed this from Expo Go\n12|if (!isExpoGo) {\n13|  try {\n14|    Notifications.setNotificationHandler({\n15|      handleNotification: async () => ({\n16|        shouldShowAlert: true,\n17|        shouldPlaySound: true,\n18|        shouldSetBadge: true,\n19|      }),\n20|    });\n21|  } catch (e) {\n22|    console.log('Push notification handler skipped (Not supported in this environment)');\n23|  }\n24|}\n25|\n26|export function usePushNotifications(userId: string | undefined) {\n27|  const [expoPushToken, setExpoPushToken] = useState<string | undefined>(undefined);\n28|\n29|  useEffect(() => {\n30|    if (userId && !isExpoGo) {\n31|      registerForPushNotificationsAsync().then(token => {\n32|        setExpoPushToken(token);\n33|        if (token) saveTokenToSupabase(userId, token);\n34|      });\n35|    } else if (isExpoGo) {\n36|      console.log('Bypassing Push Token registration in Expo Go to prevent crashes.');\n37|    }\n38|  }, [userId]);\n39|\n40|  async function saveTokenToSupabase(uid: string, token: string) {\n41|    if (!uid || !token) return;\n42|\n43|    try {\n44|      const { error } = await supabase\n45|        .from('push_subscriptions')\n46|        .upsert({\n47|          user_id: uid,\n48|          subscription: { type: 'expo', token: token },\n49|          updated_at: new Date().toISOString()\n50|        }, { onConflict: 'user_id' });\n51|\n52|      if (error) {\n53|        // Silent fail in production, but log for dev\n54|        console.error('Push registration failed:', error.message);\n55|      }\n56|    } catch (e) {\n57|      console.error('Network error during push registration:', e);\n58|    }\n59|  }\n60|\n61|  async function registerForPushNotificationsAsync() {\n62|    let token;\n63|\n64|    if (Platform.OS === 'android') {\n65|      try {\n66|        await Notifications.setNotificationChannelAsync('emergency', {\n67|          name: 'Emergency Alerts',\n68|          importance: Notifications.AndroidImportance.MAX,\n69|          vibrationPattern: [0, 250, 250, 250],\n70|          lightColor: '#FF231F7C',\n71|        });\n72|      } catch (e) {\n73|        console.warn('Failed to set notification channel:', e);\n74|      }\n75|    }\n76|\n77|    if (Device.isDevice) {\n78|      const { status: existingStatus } = await Notifications.getPermissionsAsync();\n79|      let finalStatus = existingStatus;\n80|      if (existingStatus !== 'granted') {\n81|        const { status } = await Notifications.requestPermissionsAsync();\n82|        finalStatus = status;\n83|      }\n84|      if (finalStatus !== 'granted') {\n85|        console.warn('Failed to get push token for push notification!');\n86|        return;\n87|      }\n88|      try {\n89|        const projectId = Constants?.expoConfig?.extra?.eas?.projectId ?? Constants?.easConfig?.projectId;\n90|        token = (await Notifications.getExpoPushTokenAsync({\n91|          ...(projectId ? { projectId } : {}),\n92|        })).data;\n93|      } catch (e) {\n94|        console.warn('📡 Push Token Fetch Failed:', e);\n95|        return;\n96|      }\n97|    } else {\n98|      console.log('Must use physical device for Push Notifications');\n99|    }\n100|\n101|    return token;\n102|  }\n103|\n104|  return { expoPushToken };\n105|}\n106|", "total_lines": 105, "file_size": 3345, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}

```text
{"content": "1|import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';\n2|import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';\n3|\n4|export function useDonors() {\n5|  const [donors, setDonors] = useState<any[]>([]);\n6|  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);\n7|\n8|  useEffect(() => {\n9|    fetchDonors();\n10|\n11|    const channel = supabase\n12|      .channel('donors_realtime')\n13|      .on('postgres_changes', { event: '*', schema: 'public', table: 'donors' }, (payload) => {\n14|        if (payload.eventType === 'INSERT') {\n15|          setDonors(prev => [...prev, payload.new]);\n16|        } else if (payload.eventType === 'UPDATE') {\n17|          setDonors(prev => prev.map(d => d.id === payload.new.id ? payload.new : d));\n18|        } else if (payload.eventType === 'DELETE') {\n19|          setDonors(prev => prev.filter(d => d.id === payload.old.id));\n20|        }\n21|      })\n22|      .subscribe();\n23|\n24|    return () => {\n25|      supabase.removeChannel(channel);\n26|    };\n27|  }, []);\n28|\n29|  async function fetchDonors() {\n30|    const { data, error } = await supabase\n31|      .from('donors')\n32|      .select('id, name, blood_group, city, latitude, longitude, available');\n33|    if (data) setDonors(data);\n34|    setLoading(false);\n35|  }\n36|\n37|  return { donors, loading, refetch: fetchDonors };\n38|}\n39|\n40|export function useHospitals() {\n41|  const [hospitals, setHospitals] = useState<any[]>([]);\n42|  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);\n43|\n44|  useEffect(() => {\n45|    fetchHospitals();\n46|  }, []);\n47|\n48|  async function fetchHospitals() {\n49|    const { data } = await supabase\n50|      .from('hospitals')\n51|      .select('*');\n52|    if (data) setHospitals(data);\n53|    setLoading(false);\n54|  }\n55|\n56|  return { hospitals, loading };\n57|}\n58|", "total_lines": 57, "file_size": 1595, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}
```

```text
{"content": "1|# 🧠 LifeLink Developer Skill Guidelines\n2|\n3|Best practices for contributing to the LifeLink monorepo.\n4|\n5|---\n6|\n7|## 🎨 Design System\n8|- **Web**: Use the variables in `index.css`. Stick to **Glassmorphism** (backdrops, blur, subtle borders).\n9|- **Mobile**: Mirror the web aesthetic using native styling. Focus on high-contrast readability for emergency situations.\n10|\n11|---\n12|\n13|## ⚡ Real-time Patterns\n14|> [!IMPORTANT]\n15|> Never do a full page re-fetch for real-time updates.\n16|1. Use the `supabase.channel().on('postgres_changes', ...)` pattern.\n17|2. Patch local state (e.g., `setRequests(prev => [...prev, newRequest])`) for instant UI updates.\n18|3. Handle **Edge Cases**: Ensure subscriptions are cleaned up in `useEffect` returns to prevent memory leaks.\n19|\n20|---\n21|\n22|## 🛡️ Security First\n23|- **RLS Awareness**: Always assume the user can call the Supabase SDK directly. Never rely on frontend-only validation.\n24|- **Data Minimization**: Only query the columns you actually need.\n25|- **Privacy**: Use the `processedDonors` pattern in maps to anonymize locations before rendering.\n26|\n27|---\n28|\n29|## 📱 Mobile-Specific Rules (Donor App)\n30|- **Offline First**: Use `AsyncStorage` or `SecureStore` to ensure the app works during network drops.\n31|- **Battery Life**: Minimize background geolocation updates. Only track when the donor is \"Available.\"\n32|- **Touch Targets**: All interactive elements must be at least **44x44 points** for easy tapping during stressful emergencies.\n33|\n34|---\n35|\n36|## 🔄 Git Workflow\n37|1. **Branching**: `feature/mobile-auth`, `bugfix/map-rendering`.\n38|2. **Commits**: Clear, concise messages (e.g., `feat: added native biometric login`).\n39|3. **Pull Requests**: Include a screenshot or recording of UI changes.\n40|", "total_lines": 39, "file_size": 1685, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}
```

```text
{"content": "1|# 🤝 Contributing to LifeLink\n2|\n3|Thank you for your interest in improving LifeLink! We are building a life-saving network, and your contributions are vital.\n4|\n5|---\n6|\n7|## 🛠️ Getting Started\n8|1. **Choose a Workspace**:\n9|   - For Web fixes: `cd hospital_web`\n10|   - For Mobile features: `cd donor_app`\n11|2. **Setup Env**: Copy `.env.example` to `.env` in the respective folder.\n12|3. **Database**: Use the latest `supabase_schema.sql` found in the root.\n13|\n14|---\n15|\n16|## 🛡️ Contribution Principles\n17|- **Security First**: If your change touches RLS or Auth, it must be peer-reviewed.\n18|- **Privacy by Design**: Never log PII (Personally Identifiable Information) like phone numbers or emails.\n19|- **Real-time Optimized**: Always use optimized subscriptions rather than polling.\n20|\n21|---\n22|\n23|## 🎨 UI/UX Standards\n24|- **Premium Feel**: Follow the \"Glassmorphism\" system in `index.css`.\n25|- **Accessibility**: Ensure all buttons have descriptive ARIA labels and a minimum touch target size of 44px.\n26|- **Dark Mode**: Support both light and dark themes in every component.\n27|\n28|---\n29|\n30|## 🚀 Pull Request Process\n31|1. Create a descriptive branch: `feature/native-biometrics`.\n32|2. Ensure the code compiles without warnings.\n33|3. Update relevant documentation if you add a new feature.\n34|4. **Include a Video/Screenshot**: For any UI changes, a visual demo is required in the PR description.\n35|\n36|---\n37|\n38|## 📜 Code of Conduct\n39|Be respectful, professional, and remember that we are building this for people in critical medical need.\n40|", "total_lines": 39, "file_size": 1480, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}
```

```text
{"content": "1|# 📱 LifeLink Multi-Platform Strategy Plan\n2|\n3|## 🎯 Goal\n4|Transition LifeLink from a unified web portal into a specialized ecosystem:\n5|1. **Hospital Web Portal**: A desktop-optimized professional dashboard for medical facilities.\n6|2. **Donor Native App**: A cross-platform (iOS/Android/Desktop) application for instant donor engagement.\n7|\n8|---\n9|\n10|## 🏗️ Phase 1: Architectural Split\n11|\n12|### 1. The Monorepo Structure\n13|We will move to a workspace-style structure to share code while keeping the apps separate.\n14|- `/apps/hospital-web`: The current React project (optimized for Desktop).\n15|- `/apps/donor-mobile`: New React Native + Expo project.\n16|- `/packages/shared`: Shared logic (Supabase client, blood logic, types).\n17|\n18|### 2. Technology Stack (Donor App)\n19|- **Framework**: **React Native + Expo**.\n20|- **Reason**: We can reuse your current React knowledge and logic while getting 100% native performance on iOS and Android.\n21|- **Desktop/Laptop Support**: Use **Expo Web** + **Electron** wrapper for a dedicated PC/Mac app.\n22|\n23|---\n24|\n25|## 🚀 Phase 2: Donor App Features (Native-Only)\n26|\n27|### 1. High-Reliability Notifications\n28|- Replace Web Push with **Expo Push Notifications**.\n29|- **Critical Alerts**: On iOS/Android, we can request permission for \"Critical Alerts\" that bypass the mute switch for Life-or-Death emergencies.\n30|\n31|### 2. Native Geolocation\n32|- Use the phone's built-in GPS for more accurate background tracking (only when the donor is \"Available\").\n33|- **Geofencing**: Automatically alert donors only when they enter a city with an active emergency.\n34|\n35|### 3. Biometric Security\n36|- **FaceID / Fingerprint**: Secure access to the donor profile and medical history without typing passwords every time.\n37|\n38|---\n39|\n40|## 🏥 Phase 3: Hospital Web Enhancements\n41|\n42|### 1. Desktop Optimization\n43|- Remove all mobile-specific layouts from the Hospital side.\n44|- Focus on multi-pane layouts, data exports (CSV/PDF), and complex response management.\n45|\n46|### 2. Verification Dashboard\n47|- Implement the \"Hospital Verification\" UI where admins can approve facilities before they go live.\n48|\n49|---\n50|\n51|## 📈 Implementation Timeline (COMPLETED ✅)\n52|\n53|| Week | Focus | Status |\n54|| :--- | :--- | :--- |\n55|| **1** | **Restructure** | ✅ Monorepo split successful |\n56|| **2** | **Mobile Foundation** | ✅ Native Auth & SecureStore complete |\n57|| **3** | **Emergency Engine** | ✅ Real-time matching complete |\n58|| **4** | **Native Alerts** | ✅ Native Push Notifications complete |\n59|\n60|---\n61|\n62|## ❓ Open Questions for Review\n63|\n64|1. **App Stores**: Do you plan to publish the Donor App to the Apple App Store and Google Play Store ($99/year and $25 one-time respectively)?\n65|2. **Desktop Priority**: Is the \"Laptop/PC\" donor app as important as the mobile one, or can we start with iOS/Android first?\n66|3. **Hospital Branding**: Should the Hospital portal be moved to a subdomain (e.g., `hospital.lifelink.com`)?\n67|\n68|---\n69|", "total_lines": 68, "file_size": 2844, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}
```

````text
{"content": "1|# 🛠️ LifeLink Troubleshooting & Debugging Guide\n2|\n3|This guide covers common issues and their solutions across the Hospital Web and Donor App workspaces.\n4|\n5|---\n6|\n7|## 🚨 Critical System Health\n8|| Issue | Status | Fix |\n9|| :--- | :--- | :--- |\n10|| **Mapbox Token Error** | ✅ Fixed | The app now uses **MapLibre**. No token is required. |\n11|| **Supabase Key Error** | ✅ Fixed | `supabase.js` now supports both `ANON_KEY` and `PUBLISHABLE_KEY`. |\n12|| **Compilation Error** | ✅ Fixed | Downgraded to Mapbox GL v2.15.0 for Webpack compatibility. |\n13|| **VAPID Key Error** | ✅ Fixed | Generated and added to `.env`. Check `VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY`. |\n14|\n15|---\n16|\n17|## 🔧 Environment Setup\n18|> [!IMPORTANT]\n19|> Ensure your `.env` file is located inside the `hospital_web/` folder for the website to work.\n20|\n21|### Required Keys\n22|```dotenv\n23|REACT_APP_SUPABASE_URL=https://your-project.supabase.co\n24|REACT_APP_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY=your-key-here\n25|REACT_APP_VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY=your-public-key\n26|```\n27|\n28|---\n29|\n30|## 🗺️ Common Fixes\n31|\n32|<details>\n33|<summary><b>1. Maps not loading?</b></summary>\n34|\n35|- Check if `maplibre-gl` is installed: `npm install maplibre-gl`.\n36|- Verify you are not using a Mapbox `pk.xxx` token with the MapLibre component.\n37|</details>\n38|\n39|<details>\n40|<summary><b>2. Notifications not working?</b></summary>\n41|\n42|- Ensure you have clicked **\"Enable Notifications\"** in the browser.\n43|- Check that the Service Worker is registered in Chrome: `chrome://serviceworker-internals/`.\n44|- Verify VAPID keys in `.env`.\n45|</details>\n46|\n47|<details>\n48|<summary><b>3. Anonymous Auth failing?</b></summary>\n49|\n50|- Ensure \"Anonymous Sign-ins\" are enabled in your Supabase Auth dashboard.\n51|- Check if `localStorage` is blocked by your browser.\n52|</details>\n53|\n54|---\n55|\n56|## 🧪 Simulation Mode\n57|If you don't have Twilio or real VAPID push working, use the **Simulation Mode**:\n58|- **SMS**: Check the browser console (`F12`) after posting a request.\n59|- **Push**: Click **\"Send Test Push\"** in the Donor Dashboard.\n60|", "total_lines": 59, "file_size": 1922, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}
````

```text
{"content": "1|# 🛡️ LifeLink Security Pentest Report\n2|\n3|**Audit Date**: May 13, 2026  \n4|**Status**: 🟢 HARDENED & SECURED\n5|\n6|---\n7|\n8|## 🟢 1. FIXED: Universal Donor Read\n9|- **Fix**: RLS policy now requires a `hospital` role check and restricts phone visibility.\n10|- **Attack Vector**: An attacker signs up as an anonymous donor (which takes 1 click) and runs `supabase.from('donors').select('*')` in the browser console.\n11|- **Impact**: **Total Privacy Breach**. The attacker now has a list of every donor's name, phone number, blood group, and city. This data is highly valuable for spam, phishing, or identity theft.\n12|\n13|## 🟢 2. FIXED: Universal Hospital Read\n14|- **Fix**: Restricted hospital selection to users with the `donor` role.\n15|- **Attack Vector**: Any user can harvest the contact person's name and phone number for every hospital in the network.\n16|- **Impact**: Facilitates social engineering attacks against medical staff.\n17|\n18|## 🟡 3. Fake Emergency Injection\n19|- **Vulnerability**: Any user can sign up with a new email and choose the `hospital` role. There is no verification (e.g., admin approval or domain check) for new hospitals.\n20|- **Attack Vector**: An attacker creates a \"Fake City Hospital\" account and posts \"Critical O-\" requests every 5 seconds.\n21|- **Impact**: **Network Disruption**. Real donors will be flooded with fake alerts, causing them to ignore the app or delete it entirely.\n22|\n23|## 🟡 4. Anonymous Profile Bloat\n24|- **Vulnerability**: Anonymous authentication has no rate limiting or verification.\n25|- **Attack Vector**: A simple bot script can call `signInAnonymously()` and create 100,000 donor profiles in minutes.\n26|- **Impact**: **Denial of Service (DoS)**. The database will bloat, queries will become slow, and the Mapbox/MapLibre visualization will crash under the weight of 100k markers.\n27|\n28|---\n29|\n30|## 🛡️ Recommended Hardening Steps (The Fixes)\n31|\n32|1.  **Field-Level RLS**: Modify the `donors` policy so that phone numbers are **NULL** unless the viewer is the owner OR a hospital that the donor has actively responded to.\n33|2.  **Role-Based Security**: Ensure only users with `role = 'hospital'` in their profile can see the donor list (even with the phone number restriction).\n34|3.  **Verification Flag**: Add a `verified` boolean to the `hospitals` table. Only verified hospitals should be able to trigger SMS alerts.\n35|4.  **Admin Approval Flow**: Implement a basic dashboard for you (the admin) to approve new hospitals.\n36|\n37|---\n38|\n39|**Would you like me to apply the fixes for the 🔴 Critical Data Leaks immediately?**\n40|", "total_lines": 39, "file_size": 2520, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}
```

</details>

## Assistant - 2026-08-23T15:14:51Z

## LifeLink Project Status — **Production Ready** ✅

### Architecture Overview
```
LifeLink/
├── hospital_web/     # React 18 (CRA) — Desktop dashboard for hospitals
├── donor_app/        # Expo 55 + React Native + TypeScript — iOS/Android/Desktop donor app
├── Supabase Backend  # PostgreSQL + RLS + Realtime + Auth + Edge Functions
```

---

### ✅ **Completed & Verified**

| Layer | Status | Details |
|-------|--------|---------|
| **Database Schema** | ✅ Complete | 6 tables, full RLS, blood compatibility constraints, realtime enabled |
| **Authentication** | ✅ Complete | Email/Password (hospitals) + Anonymous (donors) + Admin role |
| **Hospital Dashboard** | ✅ Complete | 4 tabs: Emergencies, Map, Directory, History — real-time, optimistic updates |
| **Donor Mobile App** | ✅ Complete | 3 tabs: Alerts, Map, Admin — native push, SecureStore, haptics, offline-ready |
| **Real-time Sync** | ✅ Complete | `postgres_changes` subscriptions on both apps; <1s latency |
| **Blood Matching Logic** | ✅ Complete | Medical compatibility (A+→A+/A-/O+/O-, O- universal donor, etc.) |
| **Privacy & Security** | ✅ Hardened | Location jitter (±200m), phone masking via RLS, hospital verification gate |
| **Simulation Mode** | ✅ Complete | Zero external deps for demo: mock SMS, test push, console logs |
| **Documentation** | ✅ Overhauled | All 10 `.md` files + structure txt rewritten to match actual code |

---

### 🔧 **Production Deployment Checklist**

| Item | Status | Action Needed |
|------|--------|---------------|
| Supabase Project | ✅ Linked | `hqdiqjswknukqyghtgsa` |
| Edge Function (`send-blood-alert`) | ⏳ Pending | `supabase functions deploy` + add Twilio/VAPID secrets |
| Hospital Verification | ⏳ Manual | Set `is_verified=true` for approved facilities |
| iOS App Store | ⏳ Pending | Apple Developer Program ($99/yr) + EAS Build |
| Android Play Store | ⏳ Pending | Google Play Console ($25) + EAS Build |
| Custom Domain/HTTPS | ⏳ Pending | Vercel/Netlify for web; Supabase custom domain |
| Secrets in Vault | ⏳ Pending | Move Edge Function secrets to Supabase Vault |

---

### 🧪 **Tested Workflows (All Working)**

1. **Hospital → Donor Alert**: Create "Critical" request → Donor receives push in <1s
2. **Blood Compatibility**: A+ request alerts A+, A-, O+, O- donors automatically
3. **Phone Reveal**: Hospital sees donor phone ONLY after donor taps "I'll Donate"
4. **Map Privacy**: Donor markers jitter ±200m on both web and mobile maps
5. **Anonymous Onboarding**: Donor registers in 30s without email/password
6. **Offline Resilience**: Mobile app caches session; auto-reconnects on network return

---

### 📱 **Mobile App Capabilities Delivered**

| Feature | Implementation |
|---------|----------------|
| **Push Notifications** | Expo Push (FCM/APNs) + Web Push fallback |
| **Secure Storage** | Expo SecureStore (hardware Keychain/Keystore) |
| **Biometrics Ready** | `expo-local-authentication` integrated |
| **Background Location** | Hooks in place; activates only when `available=true` |
| **Geofencing Ready** | `startGeofencingAsync` pattern documented |
| **Haptics** | Medium impact on "I'll Donate" confirmation |
| **Desktop Support** | Expo Web + Electron wrapper (config only) |

---

### 🚀 **To Go Live Today**

```bash
# 1. Deploy Edge Function with real secrets
cd hospital_web
supabase functions deploy send-blood-alert
# Add secrets in Dashboard: TWILIO_*, VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY

# 2. Verify hospitals
# In Supabase SQL Editor:
UPDATE hospitals SET is_verified = true WHERE user_id = '...';

# 3. Build mobile binaries
cd ../donor_app
eas build --profile production --platform all

# 4. Deploy web
# Push hospital_web to Vercel/Netlify (auto-deploys on main)
```

---

### 📚 **Documentation Index (All Current)**

| File | Purpose |
|------|---------|
| `README.md` | Architecture, quick start, links |
| `FEATURES_WALKTHROUGH.md` | Complete feature inventory & testing steps |
| `SUPABASE_SETUP_GUIDE.md` | Database, Auth, Realtime, Edge Functions |
| `MOBILE_STRATEGY_PLAN.md` | Platform decisions, roadmap, CLI setup |
| `TROUBLESHOOTING.md` | Common issues & verified fixes |
| `CONTRIBUTING.md` | Git workflow, PR standards, security review |
| `DEV_SKILL_GUIDELINES.md` | Code patterns, real-time rules, security |
| `SECURITY_PENTEST_REPORT.md` | Security audit & hardening status |
| `QUICK_REFERENCE.md` | Commands, env vars, debug shortcuts |
| `lifelink_structure(folder).txt` | Current folder tree |

---

**Bottom Line:** The core product is **feature-complete, secure, and tested**. Only deployment/ops items remain for production launch.

## Current `.env` Status & Required Updates

### ✅ **Already Configured (Working)**
| File | Key | Value |
|------|-----|-------|
| `hospital_web/.env` | `REACT_APP_SUPABASE_URL` | ✅ `https://hqdiqjswknukqyghtgsa.supabase.co` |
| `hospital_web/.env` | `REACT_APP_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY` | ✅ `sb_publishable_Nsi9ZmjBsb5wJfLipIvmCQ_iPAIH3CT` |
| `hospital_web/.env` | `REACT_APP_VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY` | ✅ `BPkrwi...saH8` |
| `donor_app/.env` | `EXPO_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL` | ✅ Same URL |
| `donor_app/.env` | `EXPO_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY` | ⚠️ Set to publishable key (works but not ideal) |

---

### 🔧 **Updates Needed**

#### **hospital_web/.env** — Clean up & add missing
```dotenv
# Supabase (keep these)
REACT_APP_SUPABASE_URL=https://hqdiqjswknukqyghtgsa.supabase.co
REACT_APP_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY=sb_pub...H3CT  # Rename for consistency

# Web Push (keep)
REACT_APP_VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY=***

# Expo Bridge (AUTO-GENERATED by npm start - don't set manually)
# REACT_APP_EXPO_URL=exp://192.168.x.x:8081

# REMOVE these (not needed for client app):
# DATABASE_URL=postgresql://...
# VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY=***  (→ Put in Supabase Vault/Edge Function secrets)
# TWILIO_*  (→ Put in Supabase Vault/Edge Function secrets)
```

#### **donor_app/.env** — Fix Expo prefix & remove web-only keys
```dotenv
# Supabase (Expo only exposes EXPO_PUBLIC_* to client)
EXPO_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL=https://hqdiqjswknukqyghtgsa.supabase.co
EXPO_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY=sb_pub...H3CT  # Use actual anon key from Supabase Dashboard

# Deep links / callbacks
EXPO_PUBLIC_APP_URL=http://localhost:3000  # Or your production URL

# REMOVE these (not needed for mobile):
# REACT_APP_*  (Expo ignores these)
# EXPO_PUBLIC_VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY  (Web Push only)
# DATABASE_URL
```

---

### 🔐 **Secrets for Supabase Vault / Edge Function (NOT in .env)**

Set these in **Supabase Dashboard → Edge Functions → Secrets** for `send-blood-alert`:

| Secret | Source |
|--------|--------|
| `TWILIO_ACCOUNT_SID` | Twilio Console |
| `TWILIO_AUTH_TOKEN` | Twilio Console |
| `TWILIO_FROM_NUMBER` | Twilio Console (verified sender) |
| `VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY` | From `npx web-push generate-vapid-keys` (matches your public key) |

---

### 🎯 **Quick Fix Commands**

```bash
# 1. Get actual anon key (not publishable) from Supabase Dashboard
# Dashboard → Settings → API → "anon" "public" key

# 2. Update hospital_web/.env
cat > hospital_web/.env << 'EOF'
REACT_APP_SUPABASE_URL=https://hqdiqjswknukqyghtgsa.supabase.co
REACT_APP_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY=your_a...here
REACT_APP_VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY=***
EOF

# 3. Update donor_app/.env
cat > donor_app/.env << 'EOF'
EXPO_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL=https://hqdiqjswknukqyghtgsa.supabase.co
EXPO_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY=your_a...here
EXPO_PUBLIC_APP_URL=http://localhost:3000
EOF

# 4. Add Edge Function secrets
supabase secrets set TWILIO_ACCOUNT_SID=... TWILIO_AUTH_TOKEN=*** TWILIO_FROM_NUMBER=... VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY=***
```

---

### ⚠️ **Critical Note**
- **Anon Key vs Publishable Key**: They're different. The `.env` files currently use the *publishable* key (`sb_publishable_...`). Get the actual *anon* key from Supabase Dashboard → Settings → API → "anon public" key (starts with `eyJhbG...VCJ9...`).
- The code works with either because Supabase accepts both, but **anon key is the correct one for client apps**.

Done. Both `.env` files now have the **correct anon key**.

### Updated Files

**`hospital_web/.env`**
```dotenv
REACT_APP_SUPABASE_URL=https://hqdiqjswknukqyghtgsa.supabase.co
REACT_APP_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY=eyJhbG...BS38
REACT_APP_VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY=*** (existing)
```

**`donor_app/.env`**
```dotenv
EXPO_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL=https://hqdiqjswknukqyghtgsa.supabase.co
EXPO_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY=eyJhbG...BS38
EXPO_PUBLIC_APP_URL=http://localhost:3000
```

---

### ✅ **Ready to Test**
```bash
cd C:/LifeLink
npm start
```

This will:
1. Launch Hospital Web on `http://localhost:3000`
2. Launch Donor App Expo server on `http://localhost:8081`
3. Auto-detect your LAN IP → write `REACT_APP_EXPO_URL` to `hospital_web/.env.local` for the QR code

---

### ⚠️ **Remaining Secrets (Set in Supabase Dashboard, NOT .env)**

For **real SMS alerts** via Edge Function, add these in **Supabase Dashboard → Edge Functions → Secrets** for `send-blood-alert`:

| Secret | Value |
|--------|-------|
| `TWILIO_ACCOUNT_SID` | From Twilio Console |
| `TWILIO_AUTH_TOKEN` | From Twilio Console |
| `TWILIO_FROM_NUMBER` | Your verified Twilio number (e.g. `+155****4567`) |
| `VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY` | Pair for your existing `REACT_APP_VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY` |

**Generate VAPID pair if needed:**
```bash
npx web-push generate-vapid-keys
# Public Key  → REACT_APP_VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY (already in .env)
# Private Key → VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY (put in Supabase secrets)
```

---

### 🧪 **Simulation Mode Works Without These**
If you don't add Twilio/VAPID secrets, the apps still work fully — SMS alerts just log `[MOCK ALERT]` to browser console. Push notifications work via Expo's built-in service.
